# Project validation history

本文件是按日期追加的跨模块验证日志。每节中的版本、数字、失败与未执行范围只对应该轮，不作为当前设计；专项模型和硬件样本在 [验证索引](README.md) 所列报告中拥有正文。相同运行的摘要与专项报告不能算作两次独立验收。








## 2026-10-20 — Merge foundation/home work with main tracking-speed history

- User requested merging the current work into main and cleaning fully merged local/remote branches. The source tip `f37a7f47` and local main tip `fe73223e` had diverged (13 source-only and three main-only commits); remote main was `c1d1f47d`. The merge preserves both histories, including main's tracking speed, motor direction and admitted-speed holding changes. `main-sh` has two additional unique commits and is excluded from merged-branch cleanup.
- One source conflict in NexArmBluetooth was resolved by retaining main's cancellation-independent restoreLive API through the source branch's lazy native/admission path and explicit error reporting. Root closure, session identity, legal packets, pacing and native capability checks remain enforced. Main-only tracking sources were normalized to repository formatting; no tracking behavior was changed for formatting.
- Integration reconciliation: the new root-cleanup fixture lacked restoreLive and now explicitly rejects it as disconnected, matching its other write methods. The manual-handoff regression originally rejected every synchronous position packet, including main's required tracking mode-exit restoration. Its fixture now records exact admitted restoration indices, preserving all writes in the log. The test permits only identified restoration packets and additionally requires all old restoration writes to precede the first new slider command; stale ordinary preset writes remain forbidden.
- PASS: final production unsigned generic iOS MobileHarness Debug build, log `/tmp/mobileharness-main-merge-device.log`; separate project/privacy/entitlement plist checks and final diff/format checks. The normal simulator build script still exits 65 because external CoreAIShared cannot resolve CoreAI for simulator architectures; later schemes are not reached, log `/tmp/mobileharness-main-merge-ios.log`.
- Full-check evidence: `/tmp/mobileharness-main-merge-check-complete.log` passes boundaries, six Python checks, strict formatting, compilation, 111 UITests and 132 AppWorkflowTests. IntegrationTests reports 1,021 tests with 24 issues, including the subsequently corrected handoff assertion plus sound-source, resource-message, playback/media and timing failures. This remains a failed full run; no full-suite or unchanged-baseline claim is made.
- PASS after reconciliation: the exact production-workflow handoff regression `bluetoothLatestSliderSupersedesPresetWhileOldAimCleanupIsJoining` passes in isolation (one test), log `/tmp/mobileharness-main-merge-handoff-focused.log`. The final test-only fixture/log assertion change was not followed by another full-suite run. Earlier integration attempts stopped at main-source formatting and then the missing fixture protocol method; logs `/tmp/mobileharness-main-merge-check.log`, `/tmp/mobileharness-main-merge-check-final.log` and `/tmp/mobileharness-main-merge-check-verified.log` retain those failures.
- No deployment, live model, camera/microphone or physical arm execution/acceptance was performed during this merge. Earlier phone installations do not establish acceptance of the newly combined main tracking changes.

## 2026-10-20 — Conditional island expansion and straight-segment-only spectrum

- HomeIslandPresentation derives targets from connecting/connected Bluetooth and real unfinished task presentation. Initial disconnected state stays compact. Disconnect/failed-as-disconnected immediately changes the glyph to gray, then uses a 150ms native animation delay plus 650ms retraction. The configured completion bound is 800ms; no delayed Task, timer or late completion callback exists. Reconnect replaces the same property animations from current presentation values. A live task retains the expanded shell while Bluetooth retracts independently; terminal task history does not keep it expanded. The right scope glyph appears only for current/unfinished task facts, including background and waiting-user tasks. Foreground filtering of execution logs cannot hide those facts.
- Playback spectrum is restricted to the capsule's bottom straight segment, inset by the 18.5pt corner radius on both sides. A hard layer mask clips glow outside that segment. The same 32-band view scales horizontally between compact 89pt and expanded 137pt straight segments with the shell spring; playback begins with a 240ms top-anchored vertical growth animation. Speaking alone does not expand the shell, and compact playback retains a compact straight-bottom spectrum. No audio capture or Bluetooth execution policy changed.
- PASS: required `./scripts/check.sh` boundaries, six Python checks and strict formatting; all 111 UITests and 132 AppWorkflowTests pass. New regressions cover corner exclusion, both straight-segment widths, Bluetooth/task/foreground combinations, configured disconnect timing, no repeated delay, reconnect and unfinished/background/waiting versus terminal task snapshots. These are presentation/geometry tests rather than actual iPhone animation timing. Log: `/tmp/mobileharness-island-state-check.log`.
- FAIL: the full check exits 1; IntegrationTests reports 1,019 tests with 21 issues including resource message, playback/device timing and sound-source cases. No full-suite or unchanged-baseline claim is made. The three plist checks skipped by fail-fast execution pass separately, as do final strict formatting of changed sources and `git diff --check`.
- PASS: final unsigned generic iOS MobileHarness Debug build, including the production header, task presentation and spectrum integration, exits 0 after the task symbol's bounds were fitted to its reserved width. Final log: `/tmp/mobileharness-island-state-device-final.log`; prior build: `/tmp/mobileharness-island-state-device-build.log`. Final isolated simulator-SDK type checking of the production native header/spectrum also passes: `/tmp/mobileharness-island-state-native-types.log`.
- BLOCKED: required `./scripts/build-ios.sh` exits 65 at MobileHarnessSimulator because external CoreAIShared cannot resolve CoreAI for simulator architectures; subsequent schemes are not reached. Log: `/tmp/mobileharness-island-state-ios.log`.
- No new installation or physical-device acceptance occurred. The phone still has the separately recorded prior installed build. Verify actual round-corner clearance, downward emergence, connecting→connected→disconnect/failure, task overlap/end, rapid reconnect, the visible gray/retraction duration, background and Reduce Motion on the target iPhone. Hardware/system island dimensions remain public-safe-area estimates; API configuration and state tests do not establish native visual acceptance or physical device execution.

## 2026-10-20 — Repeated listening activation haptic access

- Investigation: every admitted manual restart creates a fresh voice generation and feedback gate and invokes canonical cue/haptic children. NativeHapticFeedback reuses UIKit generators and releases its operation after completion. The recording path sets recording-haptic access before starting SpatialAudioCapture, which subsequently enables AVCaptureSession's automatic shared-session configuration. This makes a stale recording-haptic setting a plausible suppression point; the user's first-only physical symptom was not reproduced or instrumented on a phone in this run.
- NativeHapticFeedback now reapplies `setAllowHapticsAndSystemSoundsDuringRecording(true)` after cancellation/admission checks immediately before each pulse, then prepares and emits the impact. It does not replace category/mode/routes or stop capture. Setter failures propagate through the existing native error report; generator caching, bounded repetition and joined cancellation remain intact. Relevant Apple contracts: [recording haptic access, default false](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/avfaudio/avaudiosession/allowhapticsandsystemsoundsduringrecording) and [capture-session automatic configuration](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/avfoundation/avcapturesession/automaticallyconfiguresapplicationaudiosession).
- PASS: required `./scripts/check.sh` module boundaries, six Python checks and strict Swift formatting. All 132 AppWorkflowTests pass, including the new same-session five-activation regression that checks one cue/haptic submission and successful child report per activation using one retained fixture endpoint. This fixture does not measure physical vibration. Log: `/tmp/mobileharness-repeated-listening-haptic-check.log`.
- FAIL: the same full check exits 1. UITests reports 107 tests with one issue in `cameraPoseExpiresWithoutRemovingLiveVideo`; IntegrationTests reports 1,019 tests with 31 issues across device timing, playback, sound-source, media and related cases. No full-suite or unchanged-baseline claim is made. The three plist checks skipped by fail-fast execution pass independently; `git diff --check` passes.
- PASS: unsigned generic iOS MobileHarness Debug build, including NativeHapticFeedback, exits 0. Log: `/tmp/mobileharness-repeated-listening-haptic-device-build.log`. Final explicit iPhone SDK type check of the new native test also passes: `/tmp/mobileharness-repeated-listening-haptic-native-test-types.log`. Initial native-test checks required the SDK's Testing framework search path and testable access to an existing internal cleanup-error initializer; final source preserves primary and cleanup errors without widening production API.
- BLOCKED: required `./scripts/build-ios.sh` exits 65 at MobileHarnessSimulator because external CoreAIShared cannot resolve CoreAI for simulator architectures; subsequent schemes are not reached. Log: `/tmp/mobileharness-repeated-listening-haptic-ios.log`.
- NOT RUN: `nativeHapticRestoresRecordingAccessOnEveryRequestAndAfterStop` is iOS-only and opt-in via `HAPTIC_DEVICE_ACCEPTANCE=1` on a haptic-capable device. It reuses one native endpoint, resets recording-haptic access before five requests, verifies recovery including after stop, and restores the original shared-session value. No physical-device installation, capture or vibration acceptance occurred. Follow [Voice acceptance](Voice.md) for at least five real manual start/stop cycles, including after playback and foreground return; successful API submission alone cannot confirm actual vibration.

## 2026-10-17 — Persistent home under deep navigation and lossless scene interruption

- User reported that home audio survives Settings but Actions stop after deeper history/context navigation. Source audit found home inside NavigationStack and native expression availability tied to window attachment; deep navigation can detach that renderer and fail finite expression acknowledgments. This is a diagnosed mechanism, not an instrumented reproduction on the physical phone. ApplicationRootView now uses PersistentHomeNavigation: the live home/camera/native renderer remains a sibling beneath the navigation stack, with hit testing/accessibility transferred to covering pages. Foreground idle-timer policy is independent of navigation depth. Photo/history inspectors use public UIKit overFullScreen via a single retained presenter, preserving underlying views and real dismissal completion; no fake animation acknowledgments or business lifecycle moved into UI.
- Also found that latest-state-only Scene coalescing could omit foreground(false), or cut its service traversal short, after rapid inactive → active. ApplicationLifecycle now latches one pending suspension, completes reverse-order suspension of every started service before active admission, and retains existing immediate native invalidation, startup rollback, cancellation and failure reporting. Navigation emits no Scene suspension; returning active does not replay cancelled business work. No new directories or dependencies were introduced.
- PASS: native macOS SwiftUI/AppKit regression mounts the production navigation container, pushes five levels, pops/reopens, and verifies a single native home identity, uninterrupted window attachment/state updates/home task and cancellation on root destruction. Initial harness attempts lacked native window ordering and assumed cached destination onAppear repeats on pop; the final harness fixes those assumptions while retaining all home lifetime assertions. Final focused run passes this regression plus all 119 AppWorkflowTests, including fast foreground coalescing and suspension-join ordering. Log `/tmp/mobileharness-persistent-home-focused-final.log`. This native Mac test does not establish iPhone Core Animation, full-screen inspector interactions, voice/model or physical motion acceptance.
- Required final check.sh run: boundaries, six Python checks, strict formatting, UI 68 and AppWorkflow 119 pass; IntegrationTests fails with 18 issues across nine tests (eight sound-source cases and the over-budget vision case), out of 909 reported tests. Full-suite success is not claimed. Log `/tmp/mobileharness-persistent-home-check-final.log`. The earlier full run also failed sound-source cases and one atomic-motion timing assertion (`/tmp/mobileharness-persistent-home-check.log`); no unrelated runtime fix is claimed. Project/privacy/entitlement plist checks skipped by fail-fast check.sh pass independently. Diff formatting and changed-document local path checks pass.
- Required build-ios.sh stops at MobileHarnessSimulator because external CoreAIShared cannot resolve CoreAI for simulator architectures; later schemes are not reached. Log `/tmp/mobileharness-persistent-home-ios.log`. An initial device-specific build could not find the disconnected iPhone destination (`/tmp/mobileharness-persistent-home-device.log`); this was a destination failure, not a source compilation result.
- PASS: separate signed generic iOS Debug build 0.1.0/2026101707 using existing local signing and command-line version override (`/tmp/mobileharness-persistent-home-device-final.log`); deep/strict signature, valid target-device provisioning, all 25 Agent/Skill assets plus Core protocol/function declarations pass. The two new navigation/presentation components also pass strict iOS type checking (`/tmp/mobileharness-persistent-home-native-types.log`). All 659 captured source/configuration/resource inputs remain identical before/after final build; snapshot `/tmp/mobileharness-persistent-home-inputs-final.json`.
- NOT INSTALLED: paired iPhone 17 Pro remains unavailable after the build; no installation, app launch, data reset, native iPhone navigation/inspector/audio or physical-action acceptance occurred. Signed bundle is `build/DerivedData/Build/Products/Debug-iphoneos/MobileHarness.app`. Source signing/version settings and unrelated working-tree changes were preserved.

## 2026-10-16 — Reopening startup and caption rendering correction, installed build 2026101603

- User reported preparation failure on reopening (`ApplicationLifecycleFailure(service: nil, primary: CancellationError, cleanup: [])`), partial promoted recognition captions despite complete history, and intermittent text fade hitches. Source audit found initial nonactive state could fail before the first service, and the caption's independent onChange/task pair could leave its revised suffix at 15% opacity. The actual SpeechPerception output submits final text before sealing; its ordered signal consumer preserves that order. The native visual incident was not reproduced with an instrumented trace, so the opacity path is a diagnosed failure mechanism rather than a measured frame trace.
- Implemented: startup waits for active before starting services; inactive/background still immediately invalidate native admission. Owner-cancelled startup with successful rollback enters suspended without failure presentation; next active stops/replaces the scope. Quick transitions coalesce, and service-origin cancellation/cleanup errors remain failures. No business operation is replayed. New lifecycle tests cover preactive waiting, stop releasing the wait, fast reactivation, failed rollback and foreground producer cancellation.
- Implemented: FadingCaption receives complete current text synchronously through a native UILabel/UIScrollView surface. Both labels use the same complete layout; the revised range alone has a 250 ms CABasicAnimation.opacity. The model opacity is always one, and finalization/Reduce Motion show the full bounded value immediately. Removal/replacement cannot strand a faded suffix. Removed per-text task/yield and animated autoscroll; layout only updates on text/size changes. Pure UI range tests cover the user's phrase, correction/finalization, rapid replacement, UTF-16 emoji/combining boundaries and the 320-Character display limit. No recognition/history content change or third-party animation library was introduced.
- PASS: focused 117 AppWorkflowTests plus five caption tests; log `/tmp/mobileharness-reopen-captions-focused.log`. Full `scripts/check.sh` passes boundaries, six Python checks, strict Swift formatting and every product except IntegrationTests. IntegrationTests reports 868 tests with ten issues in five SoundSource tests; full check exits 1 and is not declared passing. Full log `/tmp/mobileharness-reopen-captions-check.log`. ## 2026-10-16 — Record before cold speech model loading

- Listening activation now validates capabilities/record permission, opens the capture epoch and waits for actual microphone PCM before canonical cue/haptic. Removed AppContainer's ASR/VAD/TTS prewarm gate and activation acoustic-tail delay. The first native PCM enables the active animation. An independent NativeCuePlayback supplies native-start acknowledgment without stopping recording; touch-down retains PCM/haptic preparation without the old output-graph warmup/discard path.
- SpeechPerception retains native PCM while VAD acquisition loads; SpeechRecognitionTurn retains committed speech while ASR acquisition loads. Native queued work is bounded by 4096 packets, 30 media seconds and 32 MiB PCM; recognition queued work by 2048 events / 30 mono seconds. Overflow rejects explicitly; cancellation joins model work and clears the capture epoch. No cue mute window discards overlapping user speech. ## 2026-10-12 — Agent request failures carry evidence into bounded retries

Shared Chat decisions can recover from HTTP/provider, stream and output-parser errors while retaining the same input, provider binding and Task. Failed model attempts use existing failed/error history fields. Retries join accepted calls before building error/partial-output/actual-outcome data, retain native call/result pairing, and share existing request/call/feedback/generation budgets. Persistence failures and unconfirmed feedback terminate explicitly. BasicSkill revision 6 and the required Assistant decisionFailure template describe the boundary.

## 2026-10-06 — Bluetooth background retention and foreground retry episodes (ADR0142)

BluetoothWorkflow now joins background control suspension while retaining healthy native connectivity. App no longer treats inactive as background. Foreground reads current native status after cleanup before reusing or reconnecting; each eligible return starts a fresh bounded episode and successful connection immediately clears attempts. Cancelled backoff consumes no connection attempt. Manual disconnect/forget still disables automatic recovery, native closure remains mandatory for unhealthy sessions and old motion is never replayed.

- Final `./scripts/check.sh` passed: boundary guards, six Python tests, strict Swift formatting, all root test targets and metadata lint. IntegrationTests passed473 tests in61.411 seconds; AppWorkflowTests passed26 (`/tmp/mobileharness-background-reconnect-check.log`). Regressions include more than three brief successful recoveries, repeated exhausted episodes followed by foreground retry, repeated rapid background/foreground events with one native connection, fresh jog after suspension, retained writer cleanup blocking new effects, deferred background native loss and cancelled backoff.
- An initial direct Swift test invocation failed because the Command Line Tools did not locate TestingMacros without the plugin path supplied by check.sh. Initial focused runs also exposed an incorrect new test assertion using the finite-write counter for live jogs; the corrected assertion checks actual liveCommands. Those intermediate failures are not final validation evidence.
- Full-Xcode unsigned generic iOS device build succeeded and all five packaged Agent configurations verified (`/tmp/mobileharness-background-reconnect-device.log`). Required `./scripts/build-ios.sh` exited65 in MobileHarnessSimulator because CoreAIShared cannot resolve simulator CoreAI for arm64/x86_64; later schemes were not reached (`/tmp/mobileharness-background-reconnect-ios.log`). Diff whitespace checks passed.

All motion/recovery fixtures are simulated; no phone installation or physical robot commands were performed. Actual inactive/background/lock-screen connection survival, queued native callback delivery and in-flight native query isolation remain unverified. No Bluetooth background mode, state restoration, signing configuration, dependency, model weight or user media was added. Original Technical_Design.docx remains unchanged.

## 2026-10-05 — Resource control ownership only (ADR0141)

ResourceCoordinator now exposes unlocked/locked(owner) from a single actual holding index. Persistent faulted/recover, separate available snapshots and CameraCoordinator's recovery flag/branch are removed. Ordinary execution and completed stop errors do not latch a resource failure. Cleanup timeout fails its requester, retains actual work, and automatically unlocks after late completion without granting/replaying that request. Per-use ResourceExecutionPermit invalidates stale App-held context; cancellation remains cancellation. Existing lifecycle closure can observe an in-flight handoff under a bounded completion subscription without stopping its future owner twice.

## 2026-10-05 — Open issue: supported iPhone FOA capture falls back to mono

Status: ✅ Native extraction fault reproduced and corrected; the user confirmed fresh direction angles on iPhone build `2026100505`. ❌ Physical axes/precision and coexistence acceptance remain pending. Initial evidence: the user's screenshot of Settings → 音频识别 → 音频检测, dated 2026-10-05 01:15, from an iPhone 17 Pro. The iOS version and exact application build were not supplied with that screenshot. The screenshot/user media is not stored in the repository.

| Reported diagnostic field | Observed value |
| --- | --- |
| Microphone | 内置麦克风 |
| Spatial capture capability | 支持 |
| Startup outcome | 空间采集启动失败，改用普通录音。 |
| Actual capture | 普通录音 · 单声道 |
| PCM status | 音频正常 |
| Sound direction | 方向不可用 |
| Diagnostic lifecycle | 检测完成 |

The capability result maps to `AVCaptureDeviceInput.isMultichannelAudioModeSupported(.firstOrderAmbisonics) == true`. The attempt nevertheless failed before spatial first-frame readiness, and the mono fallback completed. Direction is unavailable because the estimator requires validated four-channel FOA input. This is evidence of a failed App spatial capture attempt, not evidence that the iPhone lacks spatial recording capability. Apple lists Spatial Audio recording for [iPhone 17 Pro](https://www.apple.com/nz/iphone-17-pro/specs/).

The screenshot alone did not identify the failed stage: startupFailed combines native setup/running/interruption and PCM-validation failures, separately from startupTimeout. At diagnostic completion the meter is intentionally cleared, so the empty level bar does not independently establish a recording failure. Investigation therefore added bounded native-format/failure-stage metadata rather than inferring unsupported hardware or changing session configuration.

Follow-up investigation on the paired iPhone 17 Pro confirmed iOS 27.0.1 and installed build `2026100503`. Live logs reproduced spatial startup fallback, but the existing error interpolation appeared as `<private>`. LLDB inspection read `AudioIOError.invalidFormat`; that enum still merges several failure stages. A non-running native graph probe reported FOA support and accepted both input/output admission and the current graph configuration. This excludes an unconditional graph-admission rejection in that probe, not session-start or first-PCM failure. Debugger interruption delayed the UI and the App was terminated during the investigation; that termination is not evidence of the original spatial-capture fault.

Build `2026100504` adds public format/failure metadata logging without recording PCM: first ASBD/layout and first receiver rejection are logged once; native runtime errors retain domain/code and interruption notifications retain reason. The signed generic iOS build passed and was installed on the paired device. Its first requested test coincided with the Mac/device log connection dropping. After reconnecting, the user repeated detection without debugger breakpoints; the 23:42:25 device log confirmed 48 kHz, four channels, LPCM Float32/packed (`flags=9`, `bytesPerFrame=16`, `framesPerPacket=1`) and ACN/SN3D FOA (`layout=12451844`). The next message was `Spatial PCM rejected at audio buffer list: invalidFormat`, followed by mono fallback. Native FOA delivery succeeded; App extraction failed before first-frame admission.

Validation of this diagnostic change: boundary checks, strict formatting and six Python tests passed. All audio/source/readiness and audio-detection tests passed in `check.sh`; IntegrationTests ran 465 tests with one unrelated Bluetooth recovery assertion failure (`bluetoothLateNativeAimErrorIsReportedAfterRecoveryFencesTheConnection`). Its isolated retry passed. Full-script success is not claimed. `build-ios.sh` failed in its first simulator scheme at the existing unavailable `CoreAI` module dependency; later schemes were not reached. No signing settings, audio recordings or device identifiers are committed.

Public-code comparison (2026-10-05): no independently verified iPhone 17 Pro built-in-FOA real-time direction implementation was found in this search. Related repositories are useful only within their actual input contracts:

| Source | Actual implementation / relevance |
| --- | --- |
| [Apple spatial capture sample](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/avfoundation/capturing-spatial-audio-in-your-ios-app) and [output layout contract](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/avfoundation/avcaptureaudiodataoutput/spatialaudiochannellayouttag) | Audio-only native capture; add microphone input, set FOA, then add a configured output. A single FOA output is allowed. This remains the authoritative reference for our capture backend. |
| [AmbiBeam AudioRecorder.swift](https://github.com/xorgol/AmbiBeam/blob/main/iPhoneAmbixRecorder/AudioRecorder.swift) | Uses AVCaptureSession/FOA and `.videoRecording`; checks output admission after adding the input. Its capture output does not set `spatialAudioChannelLayoutTag`, and its 24-bit setting belongs to AVAssetWriter output, not the native callback format. Its useful comparison is the public CoreMedia two-pass AudioBufferList size query/extraction pattern; its graph or exported bit depth is not evidence of our failure cause. |
| [SoundCompass](https://github.com/costajohnt/SoundCompass), [session configuration](https://github.com/costajohnt/SoundCompass/blob/main/SoundCompass/Services/AudioSessionConfigurator.swift), [audit](https://github.com/costajohnt/SoundCompass/blob/main/AUDIT.md) | Uses synthesized stereo, GCC-PHAT/level cues and device calibration rather than native FOA. It deliberately avoids HFP input and documents limited directional cues; this helps route/acceptance review but cannot establish our FOA failure cause. |
| [FOA-SELD capture factory](https://github.com/way2coder/FOA-SELD-System/blob/feat/foa_recorder/FOASELD%20System/Shared/Audio/FOAAudioCaptureFactory.swift) and [engine controller](https://github.com/way2coder/FOA-SELD-System/blob/feat/foa_recorder/FOASELD%20System/Shared/Audio/FOAAudioEngineController.swift) | The iOS backend uses AVAudioEngine, retains native input format and explicitly labels missing-channel zero padding. This is not proof of built-in iPhone FOA capture. |

Root cause and correction: the old receiver allocated four AudioBuffers and always advertised 72 bytes to CoreMedia. Four interleaved channels occupy one AudioBuffer, whose reported list size is 24 bytes. A native macOS CoreMedia probe using the device's PCM representation reproduced status `-12737` (`ArrayTooSmall`) with the old 72-byte argument and success with the queried 24-byte size. This status was measured in the native probe, not read from the original iPhone error. `AudioSampleBufferReader` now queries the required list size, checks the one-to-four-buffer bound, extracts with that size and retains the backing block throughout synchronous sample access. It preserves strict layout, representation, packing, continuity and overflow rejection. Session mode, graph order and channel mapping were not changed. Float32/Int16/Int32 and the exact ACN/SN3D layout remain the implemented compatibility contract; supporting other representations requires independent evidence.

Fix validation: all four new native CoreMedia regressions passed, covering interleaved four-channel PCM, four planar buffers, rejection above the buffer bound, and thrown-error cleanup followed by another read. Final `./scripts/check.sh` passed boundaries, six Python tests, strict formatting, all root targets and plist metadata; IntegrationTests passed469 and AppWorkflowTests passed26. The signed iOS device build succeeded, and build `2026100505` was installed and launched on the paired iPhone. `./scripts/build-ios.sh` still exits65 in its first simulator scheme because CoreAIShared cannot resolve `CoreAI`; later schemes were not reached. Evidence: `/tmp/mobileharness-spatial-reader-tests.log`, `/tmp/mobileharness-spatial-fix-check.log`, `/tmp/mobileharness-spatial-fix-build.log`, `/tmp/mobileharness-spatial-fix-install.json`, `/tmp/mobileharness-spatial-fix-launch.json`, `/tmp/mobileharness-spatial-fix-simulator.log`. No signing settings, user audio or device identifiers are committed.

Physical retest: an independent installed-app query confirmed version 0.1.0/build `2026100505` (`/tmp/mobileharness-spatial-fix-installed-apps.json`). The user repeated Settings detection and reported “有角度，方向不确定，还有安静”. Active angles require actual FOA input, SpeechPerception estimation and accepted `sound.source` observations through OutputGateway; the diagnostic received those fresh observations. Uncertain/inactive transitions are also observed. This establishes restored capture-to-observation operation on this phone, not angular accuracy. The final Console view contained no retained messages; absence of an error in that view is not used as evidence of a complete error-free ten-second run. Controlled front/back/left/right axes, angular precision, repeated start/stop, route changes, camera/playback coexistence and sustained capture require separate acceptance. No Photographer or robot behavior is added by this investigation. Current architecture remains [ADR0124](../adr/0124-sound-source-perception.md) and [ADR0128](../adr/0128-settings-audio-detection.md).

## 2026-10-05 — Independent Bluetooth diagnostics implementation (ADR0131)

- SDK owns a64-event native diagnostic cache with event/connection identities, original reasons, traffic and explicit eviction counts. Persistent native send blockage detection is SDK-owned. App snapshot ingestion consumes diagnostics once, separately from native-state recovery; clearing UI history retains the consumption cursor. Device records accept only native faults and App connection/recovery stages.
- Removed execution-to-device recording/recovery catches, resource diagnostic categories and recovery's Actions dependency. Actions owns NexArmExecutionError; obsolete posture/verification/completion wrapper cases are removed. Original caught errors survive motor reports, composition, Gateway translation and home observation. Generic Output failure history is bounded100 with8 newest-one observers. Late continuous worker errors use separate home execution observation, never a rewritten Tool result.
- Home shows execution failures only in its scheduling issue list; camera tracking error banners and conversation Bluetooth readiness fallback are removed. Settings presets/reset/jog still submit immediately, reuse Actions and bypass Gateway resources; current manual errors terminate only that worker, with no UI/diagnostic error or persistent admission latch. Replacement remains cancellable.
- Migrated obsolete BLE simulations to passive connection/refresh, native-only faults, independent resource errors and configured native motor targets. Selected88 integration regressions passed before the final two tests were added; the subsequent full run also passed both new late-worker and bounded Tool-message regressions. Independent failure projection and all26 AppWorkflow tests passed; both UI connection-state regressions passed. Root checks passed architectural guards, six provisioning tests and strict Swift formatting.
- Required check.sh does not pass overall: the final433-test IntegrationTests run has6 issues across the two existing aim-planner tests and the intermittent vision deadline test. An earlier full run in this implementation had only the two aim tests failing. All Bluetooth fault/manual/recovery/coexistence regressions passed in the final full run. No complete-suite success is claimed.
- Final full-Xcode unsigned generic iOS device build passed. Required build-ios.sh remains blocked in the first simulator scheme because CoreAIShared cannot resolve CoreAI; its remaining schemes did not execute. Project/privacy/entitlement metadata and diff whitespace checks passed. No physical motion/UI acceptance, installation or commit was performed. ## 2026-10-10 — Publish/send boundary audit

- Audited production publish entry points, Agent communication Actions/compatibility ports, App fact adapters and owner-directed send/runtime receipts. No production caller uses flushPublications as acknowledgment; the barrier is for tests/shutdown only. Explicit user data-operation result waits are business completion, distinct from transport acknowledgment.
- Found and removed residual waits: App environment facts allocated request waiters and awaited application.control.finished; native model-resource observations waited for presentation completion and retried based on subscriber failure; OutputGateway awaited published-contract App observers; publication overflow awaited diagnostic callbacks.
- Publish now submits bounded background work and returns before Agent/App observer completion. Observer branches share 128-operation/256-branch/8-MiB capacity and joined shutdown; loss is recorded without publisher receipt/retry. Environment facts produce no completion acknowledgment. Root-owned ApplicationControl consumer independently executes resource-fact presentation/feedback and receiver errors. send retains target admission reports and correlated receipts; no completed effects are replayed.
- Regressions cover slow/failed App observers, independent subscriber delivery, overflow, recipient rejection, environment errors with no publisher waiter, stale fact rejection and asynchronous resource presentation. Initial checks exposed obsolete tests expecting receiver rejection as publisher failure and immediate post-publish state; fixtures now await independently observable processing, and local-invalid payload tests still expect producer validation errors.

## 2026-10-10 — All-Agent send Action-call audit

Implemented: removed automatic native Agent.send model Tool generation and the per-role exposesNativeCommunicationTools switch. Each authorized task sender receives the existing XML send Action catalog. Explicit communication Tool registration now fails catalog assembly. Static business registrations are preserved: Memory2, ApplicationControl8, other five roles0 model Tools. Internal fixed/perception Tools are separate from model exposure and unchanged. Framework communication continues to output CommunicationAction, with send admission confirmation distinct from task completion.

Validation:
- `./scripts/check.sh` passed, including672 IntegrationTests,74 AppWorkflowTests and remaining package checks/tests. All seven effective catalogs exclude communication Tools; role business Tool paths are retained; unauthorized explicit send Tool registration rejects. Specialist request/progress/input/result and Memory parent-commit tests use Action-call send and pass. Log: `/tmp/mh-send-action-check.log`. Existing model evaluation variants explicitly skipped.
- Unsigned generic iOS device build passed and all seven bundled Agent resources verified. Log: `/tmp/mh-send-action-device.log`.
- Required `./scripts/build-ios.sh` with full Xcode failed on existing Simulator CoreAIShared unresolved CoreAI dependency; not claimed passing. Log: `/tmp/mh-send-action-simulator.log`.
- An initial bare focused swift test invocation failed because the Command Line Tools Testing macro plugin path was missing; the repository check script supplies that path and its complete final run passed. Log: `/tmp/mh-send-action-tests.log`.
- Affected diff whitespace and README relative links checked. No new directories/dependencies, live API requests or device installation. Live-model/native-UI/physical acceptance remains pending.


## 2026-10-10 — Per-Agent Action/Tool duplicate registration audit

Removed only the native model Tool; scheduled openPage and the code-owned fixed navigation InternalTool remain authorized. All seven effective model catalogs now have disjoint Action/Tool paths and unique paths within each model protocol. ApplicationControl has7 business Tools, Memory2, other roles0. Cross-Agent function reuse and internal fixed command grants remain intentional, separate from model exposure; no generic framework protocol prohibition was added.

Validation: `./scripts/check.sh` passed (673 IntegrationTests,74 AppWorkflowTests plus remaining checks/tests; existing evaluation variants skipped), including model path uniqueness and navigation Action/internal Tool compatibility. Log: `/tmp/mh-model-registration-check.log`. Unsigned iOS device build passed and seven packaged Agent resources verified (`/tmp/mh-model-registration-device.log`). ## 2026-10-10 — Full source README audit

Documentation-only reconciliation of every tracked directory under MobileHarness. Each owning README was checked against its source/configuration and direct boundaries; callable grants, algorithms, state/lifecycle/failure contracts and implementation status were reconciled. The register below records all 108 documents. Downloaded model/binary-package internal directories and vendor READMEs were excluded; their boundary is documented in Resources/Models.

The counts and inspection register in this section capture the initial audit revision. The later [overall-design expansion](#2026-10-10--expanded-overall-technical-design) records the enlarged overall README and updated static counts.

### Actual verification

| Check | Result |
| --- | --- |
| Tracked directory / README coverage | 108 / 108; no missing directories or documents |
| First-level module structure | Nine; each has separate external one-level and internal diagrams |
| Mermaid syntax | 129 / 129 parsed with installed Mermaid 12.1.0; zero parse failures |
| Markdown syntax | 108 / 108 parsed as GFM with installed marked; 66 tables and all 129 Mermaid fences retained |
| Relative file references in source READMEs | 292 checked; all target paths exist |
| Incoming source-README anchors from tracked Markdown | Zero unresolved anchors after compatibility restoration; two pre-existing broken anchors also resolved |
| Parent/direct-child navigation | Checked across all 108 directories |
| Role metadata | All seven Agent JSON configurations matched for configured Action/Tool aliases, canonical paths, parameters and declared input schemas; subscriptions/communication grants retained |
| Language/structure/status scan | English visible text; no chronological/ADR/roadmap/requirements headings; no acceptance-only item marked unimplemented |
| Source/target/resource boundaries | `python3 scripts/check-modelkit-boundaries.py` passed its import, dependency, directory, value ownership, resource and retired-implementation checks |
| Diff formatting | `git diff --check` passed |
| Repository integrity | No non-Markdown tracked changes, new directories or business/interface/module changes; original Technical_Design.docx byte-identical to HEAD |

Graphs were parsed and their declared relationships reviewed against code/configuration. No native-screen rendering or runtime diagram simulation was performed. No build, automated runtime test suite, network/model inference, native UI or physical-device acceptance was run for this documentation change. The boundary checker is a static repository check, not a build or device test.

### Corrections and remaining implementation facts

Current documentation distinguishes one-way publication from directed send receipts, task completion from admission, direct Tools from Channel-scheduled Actions, effect failure from feedback-delivery failure, executor-owned results from parent task identity, local frontend state from owner-routed submitted operations, and model/native completion from physical acceptance. Historical native Assistant calls are inert request evidence; current Assistant model exposure is Action-only. Specialist history is task-scoped; SpeechPerception owns listening consent/occupancy policy; healthy camera input replacement is separate from joined vision replacement. Foundation boundaries and actual target exclusions are documented.

❗ OpenAIChat currently creates an event session with nil capacity and no producer backpressure. Its eight-MiB wire limit and per-event limit do not define a decoded-event queue capacity. Slow consumers may retain the full response; abandonment requires cancellation/join. This source-confirmed buffering gap is recorded in the [owning adapter](../../MobileHarness/ModelKit/Language/LLM/OpenAI/README.md). It was not changed in this audit.

❌ Empty/shared-extension boundaries and absent providers remain explicit: PlatformKit/Core has no compiled implementation; judgment has contracts but no production provider; System.Runtime.runtimeInfo is a fixed compatibility stub rather than live diagnostics. Other absent capabilities are listed only in their owning current designs. Lack of real-model/native/device acceptance is not treated as absence of code.

### Relocated historical claims

The following observations occurred in pre-audit source READMEs and are retained here as historical claims, not newly executed verification. Existing dedicated measurements/validation records remain the primary evidence where available.

- Former Hub README recorded Mac production Silero downloads against official Hugging Face in 11.55 s and HF-Mirror in 9.89 s, plus 38 Management/five Hub contract tests, repository checks, four simulator builds and a signed device build. These small-file durations do not establish sustained bandwidth or lock-screen/server-resume behavior.
- Former Management README recorded an early 2026-10-03 check blocked by missing Metal/full Xcode, followed by full-Xcode checks/builds and a metadata-only bundle of 104 KiB. A native YOLO storage probe recorded 3261955 installed bytes, settled transfer state, legacy /var-to-/private/var receipt-path repair and rejection of altered files. These are historical environment/probe observations, not this audit's runtime results.
- Former Gemma README recorded 2026-10-04 Mac generation returning a Chinese greeting, cancellation/unload/reload and four Gemma tests; a signed iPhone build/resource check passed. The then-current full check failed two IntegrationTests aim-follow cases (four assertions), and simulator build-ios.sh was blocked by missing CoreAI SDK. No new physical local-model inference claim follows from those records.
- Former Models README recorded local deletion of unused CoreAI Kokoro/iPhone Nemotron artifacts (about 1.6 GiB) and separate Kokoro CoreML comparison artifacts (about 330 MiB), while other ignored Mac fixtures were retained. This is cleanup history, not a required installation state.

## 2026-10-11 — OpenAI 128-event backpressure

The OpenAI Responses adapter now uses a 128-event session with `maximumEventBytes: 65568`. Serialized `yieldSuspending` covers text, native calls, usage and the final completion event. The request retains its operation lease while waiting for queue capacity. Normal completion drains accepted events; cancellation/failure wakes pending production. Consumers must drain or cancel before ordinary unload, which waits for active use. The eight-MiB wire cap remains separate from decoded-event buffering; no total process-memory bound is claimed. This resolves the nil-capacity gap recorded by the earlier source README audits.

- ✅ Focused offline Swift tests passed: 23 OpenAIChatTests and 17 ModelKitTests. Long-reply fixtures cover 127, 128 and 1,024 text fragments with delayed/slow consumption, terminal delivery, paused-consumer cancellation/shutdown and reuse after cancellation/unload. Shared session tests cover a full-buffer producer wait and cancellation wakeup. Log: `/tmp/mh-openai128-focused.log`.
- ✅ Required `./scripts/check.sh` exited 0: module-boundary checks, six Python tests, strict Swift formatting, all Swift test targets and project/privacy/entitlement plist checks passed. The full run includes the 23 OpenAIChatTests, 17 ModelKitTests and 74 AppWorkflowTests; normally gated real-model/physical cases remain skipped. Log: `/tmp/mh-openai128-check.log`.
- ❌ Required `./scripts/build-ios.sh` with full Xcode exited 65 at the first Simulator scheme: external CoreAIShared could not resolve the CoreAI module for arm64/x86_64. Later script schemes did not run. This reproduces the previously recorded Simulator dependency limitation. Log: `/tmp/mh-openai128-ios.log`.
- ✅ Unsigned generic iOS device-target Debug build passed with Xcode 27 via `xcodebuild -project MobileHarness.xcodeproj -scheme MobileHarness -configuration Debug -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' -derivedDataPath build/DerivedData CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO build`. All seven packaged Agent configurations matched their source files. Log: `/tmp/mh-openai128-device.log`. The app was not installed.
- ✅ Current adapter/module/overall designs and the owning test README describe the new bound and suspension policy. Static source README validation found all 108 documents and no path/anchor errors; Swift formatting and `git diff --check` passed. The original overall heading structure is preserved.
- No live provider, real-model, native UI, memory/latency measurement, phone installation or physical-device acceptance was performed. The first focused attempt failed only because the URLProtocol fixture did not retain `httpBody` for selecting reply length; selecting the fixture through the request URL fixed the test, and the final focused run passed.


## 2026-10-11 — Role-owned prompt templates and shared Function Token guidance

Four chat roles now own separate `.prompts.json` resources: Assistant, Photographer, Memory and ApplicationControl. Identity, response style, speech/action examples and workflow system/user messages are authored there. Task summaries, active-task context, execution observations, inert native history, photo state/review and title generation are rendered rather than embedded in Swift. The independent AgentCore resource owns generic Function Token syntax, Channel/wait semantics and catalog formatting. Native Tool declarations remain separate in `ChatRequest.tools`.

The Foundation-only renderer uses schema version 1, exact named-variable matching and single-pass substitution, with 64 templates, 64 KiB document/output and 16 KiB per template. Role discovery caches at most 32 immutable template sets without a hard-coded role list. Invalid/missing resources, templates or variables propagate errors. Configuration loading remains bundled and immutable; no template hot reload or expression engine was introduced. Existing Agent declaration JSON, parsing/execution mechanics and queue/lifecycle bounds are unchanged. Shared send examples now select a real allowed target/kind pair and omit taskID for request examples.

- ✅ Final required `./scripts/check.sh` exited 0: module-boundary checks, six Python tests, strict Swift formatting, all Swift test targets and project/privacy/entitlement plist checks passed. Includes 680 IntegrationTests and 74 AppWorkflowTests; gated physical/real-model cases remain skipped. Log: `/tmp/mh-prompts-final-check.log`. Earlier focused rendering/prompt/history/title tests and the first full check also passed before the final asset-discovery/example refinement.
- ✅ New prompt regressions cover malformed schema/expressions, exact variable contracts, UTF-8/file/template/output bounds, missing resources/templates, single-pass insertion, independent role identity/grants, shared grammar inclusion, workflow/title data and valid communication example grants. Existing parser, wait execution, continuation, title and photography tests remain in the full suite.
- ✅ Unsigned generic iOS device-target Debug build passed using Xcode 27. Final app verification matched all seven Agent declarations, four role prompt assets and one independent AgentCore protocol asset against source bytes. Offline verifier checks additionally accepted exact bundles and rejected missing, stale and unexpected prompt resources. Log: `/tmp/mh-prompts-device.log`; verifier: `scripts/verify-app-agent-resources.py`. No phone installation occurred.
- ❌ Required `./scripts/build-ios.sh` with full Xcode exited 65 at the first Simulator scheme because external CoreAIShared could not resolve CoreAI for arm64/x86_64. Subsequent script schemes did not run. This reproduces the previously documented Simulator dependency limitation. Log: `/tmp/mh-prompts-ios.log`.

Mermaid parsing passed for all 157 graphs; GFM parsing passed for all 108 tracked source READMEs and 92 tables. | [Support/Context](../../MobileHarness/Agents/Support/Context/README.md) | Repository and document adapter; distinguish optional in-memory persistence, FIFO cancellation behavior and mutation-owner cancellation policy. |
| [Support/Core](../../MobileHarness/Agents/Support/Core/README.md) | Foundation-only target/contracts and resource packaging; integrate protocol rendering ownership and reflect Capability/Generation source use within one target. |
| [Core/Capability](../../MobileHarness/Agents/Support/Core/Capability/README.md) | v6 decoder, registry, documentation and builder; add context-construction flow, sorted catalog/render bounds and separate native definitions/internal grants. |
| [Core/Communication](../../MobileHarness/Agents/Support/Core/Communication/README.md) | Policy, task scope/registry, delivery history and trace values; document opaque task data versus encoded envelope bounds and status/provenance checks. |
| [Core/Generation](../../MobileHarness/Agents/Support/Core/Generation/README.md) | Incremental generator/parser, sentence/Channel/Tool consumers, trace and new template engine; distinguish native calls from XML events and document exact template schema, variables, limits and cached resources. |
| [Core/Input](../../MobileHarness/Agents/Support/Core/Input/README.md) | AudioInput/ImageInput validation; add RGB/attachment dimensions, pixel cap, time and rotation constraints. |
| [Core/Output](../../MobileHarness/Agents/Support/Core/Output/README.md) | Calls, envelopes, mailbox, receipts/results and resource values; correct envelope versus executor identity binding, scheduling-ID precedence and shared mailbox overflow/terminal semantics. |
| [Core/Runtime](../../MobileHarness/Agents/Support/Core/Runtime/README.md) | Manager/registry, context, inbox/gate, round coordinator and budget; clarify bounded priority/FIFO batches, owner-scoped replacement keys and expiry accounting. |

## Startup publication and Responses task-argument regression

Screenshots showed Assistant rejecting startup input with notRunning and a foreground startTask carrying target=photographer, businessContract=photography, payload={}. The foreground state machine correctly rejects those background fields; the wire declaration previously omitted strict, allowing Responses to normalize optional fields into strict required fields. This is a plausible source of the model filling every field, supported by the actual request encoding and [official function-calling guidance](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/function-calling); no live GPT-4.1 request was used to prove the model's behavior.

Changes: explicitly encode strict=false for Responses functions, preserving caller-owned optional fields; strengthen canonical startTask docs and BasicSkill revision 2 to omit background fields for local foreground photography. Invalid mixed parameters still fail atomically instead of being silently stripped. Publish checks recipient running/paused lifecycle before receive; unready recipients and notRunning races remain diagnostics without publicationFailure or Chat agent.error. Directed send still returns notRunning rejection. No new queue, replay, provider retry or API Session is introduced.

- Generic iPhone unsigned Debug build passed and final App resources matched all 25 assets plus Core protocol. Log: `/tmp/mh-startup-task-device.log`.
- `./scripts/build-ios.sh` failed in its first Simulator scheme: external CoreAIShared cannot resolve CoreAI. Later schemes did not run. Log: `/tmp/mh-startup-task-ios.log`.

## 2026-10-12 — Lifecycle-owned conversation and audio

The user explicitly removed the previous 30/60-second design. Production no longer has Agent generation/queue/feedback deadlines, default Signal or communication TTL, ASR finalization/whole-turn timers, TTS Agent timers, reply-occupancy leases, PCM/error/read TTLs, native Apple ASR/TTS/startup/playback watchdogs, default Function/Tool execution deadlines or resource-cleanup acknowledgment deadlines. Explicit function timeout/estimatingDuration remains and reports through ActionResult after joined cleanup. Natural network timeout/error remains provider-owned. Perception freshness and explicitly requested device safety leases are separate from conversation lifetime.

Audio handles are single-use and bounded; playback drains and releases them, and input/session termination clears PCM/error metadata. Final-ASR rejection or failed utterance publication releases that input's pending listening hold; actual Agent occupancy requires terminal facts. ActionResult retries retain pending results until accepted/closed without time/retry disposal, and never replay effects. Queue, call and memory capacity checks remain.

Observable regressions: open generation survives 31 seconds then cancels on explicit stop; undeclared Speech Action plays for 31 seconds successfully; VAD continuous speech exceeds sixty seconds; native readiness waits for PCM/error/cancellation; synthesis/audio reads wait for owner cancellation; scoped cleanup preserves other replies; resource takeover/cancellation joins actual cleanup and keeps writers locked until then.

- Five initial lifecycle regressions passed (`/tmp/mh-lifecycle-timeout-regression.log`); four scoped cancellation/cache regressions passed (`/tmp/mh-lifecycle-focused.log`); ten native/explicit-Action regressions passed (`/tmp/mh-lifecycle-native-regression.log`); three resource cleanup regressions passed (`/tmp/mh-lifecycle-resource-cleanup.log`). No physical device or new real-model acceptance performed.
- `./scripts/check.sh` passed at the intermediate lifecycle-only stage (`/tmp/mh-lifecycle-check-verified.log`). Later whole-workspace runs encountered concurrent ADR0214 ReACT migration compile/test changes; those results do not certify the final tree. An obsolete cross-input serialization assertion was corrected to the existing task-local Channel contract while explicitly verifying cleanup join and suppression of the cancelled input's pending Actions.
- Latest generic physical-iOS build after native audio changes passed (`/tmp/mh-lifecycle-native-device.log`); final resource-cleanup build is tracked separately. `./scripts/build-ios.sh` failed external CoreAIShared simulator imports (`/tmp/mh-lifecycle-ios-verified.log`). Remaining simulator schemes were not run.

## 2026-10-12 — Consecutive foreground Task entry

The reported same-session second photograph exposed an ambiguous prompt boundary: BasicSkill treated unspecified short operations as temporary Skill loads, although photography has a save/discard decision. BasicSkill revision 10 and Photography revision 9 now require a Task for capture, permit another Task after earlier completion, distinguish camera-only controls, and specify explicit replacement as finishTask → closed result → startTask. startTask still rejects every existing foreground Task, including finishing; state is unchanged on rejection. No implicit overwrite or new parameter was added. finishTask may close an explicitly replaced goal with an honest summary; it does not prove abandoned effects succeeded.

The shared Chat worker previously returned immediately after finishing/cancelling. It now closes/adopts the default snapshot and retains finishTask business-result reasoning so the same input can advance a replacement goal. cancelTask remains terminal. Closure still joins admitted effects/replies before restoring the default catalog. Native/Action types, capacity and request budgets remain unchanged. This means an ordinary finish can have an additional result decision; no result is fabricated or device action replayed.

✅ check.sh passed boundaries, six Python tests, formatting, all test targets and project metadata; IntegrationTests reported 716 tests passing. Log: /tmp/mh-consecutive-task-check.log. After extending the synthetic workflow with a second capture in the same session, the final focused rerun passed both the closure-state test and the complete consecutive-photography workflow (0.532 seconds): /tmp/mh-consecutive-task-focused.log. Coverage includes rejection during finishing without state changes, waiting-user closure, distinct new IDs, same-input replacement association and a new-input photo with retained history. Synthetic outputs exercise framework behavior, not model choice accuracy.

✅ Unsigned physical-iOS target build passed and 25 packaged Agent/Skill assets verified: /tmp/mh-consecutive-task-device.log. ❌ Required build-ios.sh still stops at MobileHarnessSimulator/CoreAIShared because CoreAI is unavailable to that simulator dependency; later schemes not reached: /tmp/mh-consecutive-task-ios.log. No installation, physical photography/audio test or commit was performed in this fix. Real GPT-4.1 regression remains pending the original account's credit restoration; no API call was made.

## 2026-10-12 — Task context boundary and immutable photo ownership

Implemented model-only context contraction without rewriting stored conversation history. Default requests retain ordinary conversation and at most ten terminal Task summaries from the current conversation (goal/status and bounded reportedSummary/failure); archived conversations' Tasks are excluded. Summaries derive from the same persisted terminal Task records, add no model request and are historical data rather than proof of effects. Active Task history is selected per immutable model-request taskID, not by rebinding a whole input/turn.

ReACT stores the originating request Task identity. On a Task boundary, requests are rebuilt from current context, the original input and the last real Task control receipt. Old observation/image messages and native call/result blocks are removed together. Same-Task continuation preserves native pairing. Continuation input evidence is also projected from the boundary receipt, preventing later history reconstruction from reintroducing raw old observations. Default decision metadata no longer inherits a closed Task from old input bindings. The duplicate recent-outcome fact injection was removed.

Assistant photo ownership values are now immutable producing Task IDs, taken from capture-round metadata. Registration rejects stopped/replaced ownership, does not reassign old photos and retains the existing bounded map. save/ignore validate current Task identity directly; camera device facts remain available, but displayed/saved IDs are projected only for current-task-owned photos. The UI preview need not be erased. Assistant model takePhoto also rejects before execution when no active bound Task exists. This does not add an authorized cross-task historical-photo retrieval workflow or migrate unknown old photo ownership into a new Task.

✅ Final check.sh passed boundaries, six Python regressions, formatting, all test targets and metadata (/tmp/mh-task-isolation-delivery-check.log). Regression verifies same-session finish→new capture, absence of the old photo ID in new requests, terminal-summary/default separation, unchanged old model-request evidence, and rejection of an old photo even after the same input is rebound. The no-Task capture guard and device-lens facts are checked. Earlier focused pairing/task runs passed; no live provider requests were performed.

✅ Final unsigned physical-iOS build passed (/tmp/mh-task-isolation-verified-device.log), including the final AssistantProcessing source; all 25 bundled Agent/Skill resources verified. ❌ Required build-ios.sh remains blocked by CoreAIShared's missing CoreAI import under the simulator SDK (/tmp/mh-task-isolation-ios.log); later schemes were not reached. No installation, physical camera/audio or real GPT-4.1 acceptance performed. Real-model tests remain pending the original account's credit restoration. No new directory, dependency, timeout, model-summary request or commit added.

## 2026-10-12 — Terminal finishTask without replaying user input

Screenshots showed a default-mode model request after successful save/finish, carrying the already-handled user text “保存。” and the earlier finishTask committed/finishing receipt (mode task and photography still loaded). The model reasonably read a new save request with no active Task. The prior unconditional finish-result continuation caused this duplicate decision.

finishTask now ends the decision after the existing execution/reply barrier by default, as cancelTask already does. Optional continueDecision=true is accepted only for continuing a different remaining goal explicitly requested in the same input; invalid values reject atomically, default is false. The flag is declared by the owning Action and stored on the closing Task; startTask still cannot overwrite an active/finishing Task. BasicSkill revision 12 and Photography revision 11 teach final confirmation in the finishing response, and explicit replacement continuation rather than ordinary save/discard follow-up.

For an explicit boundary continuation, model transition data is derived after closure from the actual terminal Task and current default/base directory. It no longer forwards the pre-barrier mode-task receipt as current state. Original ActionResult remains unchanged in history as evidence of the committed closure request. No keyword/intent heuristic, network retry, timeout or implicit Task replacement was added.

✅ Five focused regressions passed (0.297 seconds): normal photography save/finish with no extra request, same-session new capture, explicit replacement through closed/default state then a new Task, strict opt-in validation and canonical Action completion. The sound diagnostic that failed under the complete suite also passed in this focused run; this does not make the full check pass. Log: /tmp/mh-finish-terminal-verified-focused.log.

## 2026-10-13 — Unified listening interruption and terminal cancellation/Agent failures

Scope: logical listening close is also interruption of the current interaction; native reply-policy/playback pause remains distinct. Pure Action/Tool cancellation is retained terminal evidence, never an incident or ReACT trigger. Agent provider/generation/parser/observation failures terminate the input without automatic retry; actual business failure/value continuation remains supported without new cumulative limits.

Implemented: VoiceSessionCoordinator coalesces immediate native/presentation fences and concurrent policy, model/Action/Task and audio-handle cancellation, joining before a new generation. Active/waiting Tasks retain optional session ownership and cancel separately from failure. Gateway owns direct Tool/communication cancellation and late-input fences, joins producer cleanup, and preserves wrapped cancellation/mixed actual failures. Pause emits the original-round cancellation terminal. Native history and Action presentation classify cancellation consistently. BasicSkill revision18 and Assistant prompts remove Agent retry instructions/decisionFailure; existing photo/authorization behavior is preserved.

## 2026-10-14 — Model-only native photo compression

- ApplicationRuntime's owned-photo observation reader prepares JPEG data using PlatformKit NativeImageCompression before constructing ChatImage. App limits are long edge 1568 pixels, total 1,048,576 pixels, encoded JPEG 1 MiB and initial quality 0.82. Smaller upright JPEGs already meeting the budgets bypass re-encoding; larger/rotated images use Image I/O transformed thumbnails and bounded encoding attempts. Cancellation joins native work off MainActor. ReACT retains the prepared attachment; camera storage, history photo reads and Photos import still use original capture bytes. No model provider, detail=auto, credential or network behavior changes.
- Official documentation reviewed: OpenAI images/vision describes model/detail-specific resizing; Claude vision recommends client resizing and documents its standard 1568-pixel long edge; Alibaba vision recommends reasonable client resizing; Apple recommends Image I/O transforms to minimize memory impact and exposes native thumbnail/quality APIs. The 1 MiB encoded ceiling and approximately one-megapixel policy are application latency/scene-feedback defaults, not a universal vendor recommendation. Source links are in App/Composition and PlatformKit/Vision READMEs.
- Focused synthetic native tests passed seven reported tests, including four geometry cases and two actual Agent round-barrier/ReACT paths: /tmp/mh-model-photo-final-focused.log. Coverage includes pixel/byte bounds, aspect ratio/full composition, EXIF rotation, no upscale, PNG conversion, unsupported/malformed input, invalid limits, bounded byte-budget reduction/exhaustion and cancellation. Storage reopen preserves exact original bytes; offline Responses encoding contains the prepared JPEG under the original photo identity. The extra business continuation reuses that exact attachment with one photo read/preparation. No API calls or user media are used.
- Required scripts/check.sh with full Xcode passed boundary checks, six Python tests, strict formatting and compilation for its captured snapshot. The 793-test IntegrationTests run failed with 21 issues in sound-source and vision wait/timeout tests; the new compression/photo continuation tests passed. Other package targets, including 96 workflow tests, passed; model evaluation was skipped. Full check is not reported as passing: /tmp/mh-model-photo-check.log.
- An independent ID migration began changing the shared worktree during verification. Required scripts/build-ios.sh failed in the first simulator scheme with unresolved Identity imports; later schemes were not reached: /tmp/mh-model-photo-simulator.log. After Identity sources/dependencies appeared, latest focused compilation and generic iOS device compilation were blocked by UUID/RecordID mismatches in NexArmBluetooth.swift: /tmp/mh-model-photo-current-focused.log and /tmp/mh-model-photo-device.log. The new test's identities were adapted to the current interface; composed integration and iOS compilation of that evolving worktree remain unverified. Other migration changes were preserved.
- Standalone optimized compilation of the current NativeImageCompression.swift succeeded independently of the migrating modules. Ten sequential synthetic Mac conversions of a 4000×3000 two-color JPEG produced 1182×887 pixels, reducing 191,197 bytes to 20,869 bytes; measured min/mean/max were 21.57/31.87/110.85 ms including cold first use: /tmp/mh-model-photo-native-benchmark.log. This confirms native execution and bounds for one synthetic sample, not real-photo quality, iPhone latency, GPU JPEG acceleration or a universally fastest implementation. New source/test formatting passes; whole-worktree formatting/diff results during the unrelated migration are separate. No app installation or physical acceptance was performed.

## 2026-10-15 — SQLite business-record migration

- ✅ GRDB.swift7.11.1 is private to Storage; App owns records.sqlite initialization, WAL/foreign keys/synchronous FULL and one startup recovery pass. dialogs/messages/tasks/media_files/devices use independently committed AUTOINCREMENT keys; external references use their matching prefixes. Runtime request/call/scope/audio/lease/revision identities use UUID or original opaque protocol strings. No source directory/module was added and no old records are imported.
- ✅ User admission, each completed Assistant generation and each completed ReACT observation commit separately. Delta data stays in memory. SQL client UUID/role uniqueness makes retry idempotent; dirty inputs/window-bounded fingerprints limit writes to changed messages. Lists use separately stored240-character previews and metadata-only projections; no full body/recovery blob is read for a preview. Large-body detail and bounded fingerprint regressions are covered. Terminal cancellation/failure saves received text and real state. Running Task creation precedes activation; startup marks unfinished work interrupted without replay. ## 2026-10-15 — ORM identity-first records and memory-only Agent state

- Task/message identity rows commit before references escape. Managed model admission obtains message_N before invoking ChatCapability, with empty durable response/evidence until that message finishes. Every App/diagnostic role now uses memory state; contextDirectory, stored/document factories, ContextDocumentAdapter and File Context fixtures are removed. Source guards reject their return. Task/Skill/registry/view state is no longer duplicated in AssistantConversationRecovery. Fresh runtime does not replay work; SQLite startup closes unfinished identities and derives source-linked interrupted Task compression from normalized rows. Sourceless Tasks retain terminal rows without invalid empty compression; empty uncommitted user identities remain visible in history but do not enter model context. Existing settings/preferences/transfers/media/Keychain/weights and unrelated changes remain unchanged.
- Identity metadata stays immutable at terminal save: original date/history order and PK survive retry DTO changes. Record-lifecycle-v2 adds partial active-message/Task indexes through domain-supplied named migrations. Captured failure/cancellation checkpoints remain joined, idempotent and retryable; no transaction spans model/file/native awaits. A32MiB logical evidence budget replaces reliance on an incidental file-size limit; overflow preserves the prior runtime snapshot without encoding it. Collection/role-specific bounds remain.

## 2026-10-15 — Current-dialog publication recovery and write preconditions

- SQL archive/new-current rotation remains one transaction; rotation now checks Agent ownership. ConversationHistory retains a pending synchronization fence after successful SQL rotation and clears it only after the database current/window publishes to authoritative memory. Read-current, user/model admission and rotation check current at operation boundaries; healthy deltas perform no SQL access. A mismatch with active/dirty work rejects without discarding it; a clean mismatch reloads the durable current and invalidates stale read tokens. The existing transactional ensure-current recreates a missing current, and failures propagate without a fabricated draft. Paging recovery/dirty-input publication share the history gate.
- SQL identity reservation requires an unarchived dialog. Checkpoint transactions reject new message rows in archived dialogs, but allow late updates to existing identities. A mixed batch containing an invalid archived insertion rolls back its earlier updates. Dialog checks select only small identity/status columns, omitting recovery data. The misleading currentExists helper is replaced by exact-ID comparison; new shared RecordStorageError.conflict reports invalidated preconditions. No directory/schema/dependency/configuration migration, legacy importer, installation or commit was added.

## 2026-10-15 — Automatic history paging with scoped retry

- Replaced all three manual load-more surfaces with one UI-local lazy-list footer: archive dialogs, main conversation timeline and expanded Task sources. The footer's view-owned task follows cursor/retry/completion changes; it cancels on disappearance/collapse. Starting a request never changes the completion revision; completion/cancellation re-arms a footer that reappeared while an older cancelled request still held its slot, without polling or cancelling the new request itself. Errors stay beside their own footer with manual retry, preserving already loaded content.
- HistoryPageLoading tracks one active UUID ticket and attempted cursor, bounds rendered errors to2048 characters, rejects overlapping/same-cursor automatic attempts, permits explicit retry and cancellation re-entry, and ignores old-ticket completion. Each timeline has one reader, Task pages retain the existing sixteen-reader bound with explicit overflow feedback, and the archive workflow retains one shared operation slot. Archive reads validate decreasing canonical dialog IDs; timeline/Task reads reject a non-advancing next cursor. EOF hides the footer. No queue, storage schema/query, business Task lifecycle, new directory/module/dependency or compatibility code was added.
- ConversationHistoryControlling.loadMoreArchives now takes an explicit retry flag; the sole workflow implementation owns archive paging state, resets it on successful refresh, checks cancellation/current cursor before append and rejects malformed/non-progressing pages without looping. All three surfaces preserve existing Task compression/source metadata merging, full raw-detail reads, JSON display exports and navigation.
- Focused state/workflow tests cover duplicate/in-flight admission, manual retry after a failure, next-cursor progression, refresh, EOF, invalid archive cursor, cancellation with both a late returned page and a late thrown error, and stale-ticket rejection (/tmp/mh-auto-history-focused-final.log). ## 2026-10-15 — Native history pull-to-refresh

- Archive and conversation timelines use native List.refreshable. Automatic paging footers use the native ProgressView with an accessibility label, without custom refresh/insert animations. Timeline refresh retains expansion/navigation, cancels and joins its one main reader and up to sixteen Task readers before resetting tickets, and keeps visible records on failure. Refresh does not cancel business mutations or Agent work. No database/schema, Task lifecycle, model context or compatibility changes.

| 首次全量 check | ❌ IntegrationTests 891 项中 9 项失败、17 个 issue：声源 7 项、机械臂截止时间 1 项、原生视觉统计 1 项。该次运行在最后一项关闭接纳测试加入前。 |

| 失败隔离复查 | ✅ 最终失败的 6 项声源用例分别使用 `swift test --skip-build --filter` 运行，全部通过；不据此改判全量结果，也不宣称已修复原有并发/时序问题。 |
| ./scripts/build-ios.sh | ❌ 首个 MobileHarnessSimulator scheme 在外部 coreai-models/CoreAIShared 缺少 CoreAI 模块处失败，与既有限制一致；后续独立 scheme 未执行。 |
| iOS 设备目标 | ✅ 完整 Xcode 下，MobileHarness、Debug、generic/platform=iOS、CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO，最终源码构建成功。 |

## 2026-10-17 — State public register and useState

Base: `ef19b04a`, branch `codex/storage-lifecycle-boundary`; implementation remains uncommitted. No merge, installation, persistence-format change or new third-party dependency.

- AgentContextRepository now owns its sole private actor value; Task/Skill registries, scheduling, counters and input evidence no longer register in shared State. Explicit post-commit public projection preserves committed private/durable facts on publication failure and invalidates stale public reads.
- StorageState supports declared names, application/Agent-session addresses, typed values plus atomic encoded projections, bounded read-only snapshots and publication invalidation. Existing version/generation/watch semantics remain; watch does not invoke models or Actions.
- Camera/Robot owners synchronously publish finite facts. Camera running is confirmed by native camera queries rather than preview visibility; action acceptance and physical completion remain distinct. Task exposes only goal/running/waitingUser, keeps the last business view during closing and becomes null on terminal commit. No waitingFor or new task workflow.
- All bundled Skills use schema v2, incremented revisions and required useState. Each actual Skill-backed request, including ReACT, rebuilds one CURRENT STATE system block from a fresh scoped snapshot; real sent messages are recorded in modelRequests.requestJSON. Removed Assistant camera mirror and old task/Skill/camera state-user injection/templates, including the separate Task-transition state summary. Task boundary reconstruction now reuses actual standard Action observations; the explicit Task replacement regression passes. Native Tool pairing, Action results and historical evidence remain.

## 2026-10-17 — Prompt size, State placement and cache usage evidence

- At the user's request, first committed the preceding working tree as `4edff0f3` (`refactor(state): publish finite facts and preserve assistant sessions`) on `codex/storage-lifecycle-boundary`. The prompt optimizations below follow that checkpoint and remain uncommitted. No merge or installation.
- BasicSkill revision23 consolidates shared Task/Skill, speech and failure teaching; Photography revision22 retains camera-specific readiness, capture, consent and partial-success rules. Removed obsolete teaching to read private skillOwnership/loadedSkills facts. Base Skill residency, function declarations, Action/native Tool division and required device-result boundaries remain unchanged.
- Measured teaching bodies against the checkpoint: BasicSkill 12,967 → 5,710 characters (55.97% smaller; UTF-8 13,517 → 5,858 bytes); Photography 9,994 → 4,865 characters (51.32% smaller; UTF-8 10,416 → 5,041 bytes). The assembled static system in the budget fixture is 19,826 UTF-8 bytes for base Skills and 26,260 with photography, excluding State and history. These are text measurements, not tokenizer, billed-token or cost measurements.
- Each Skill-backed request retains stable instructions/catalog in the first system message and freshly reads State into a unique separate system message near the request tail: before the latest user/Action observation, or after a complete native Tool result group. Continuations rebuild this block rather than retaining old facts. Exact provider-neutral request evidence preserves this ordering. Gemma4 explicitly joins all system bodies in source order into its single leading system turn; local cache is still reset per request.
- Pure Responses SSE decoding retains optional cached_tokens/cache_write_tokens through model usage, stored request evidence and Generation Meta. Missing is distinct from zero; malformed counts are rejected. Existing evidence without the optional fields remains readable; no SQL migration, network/credential configuration or live API call. No model-specific explicit cache breakpoint is configured and no cache hit is guaranteed by this layout.

## 2026-10-17 — XML Action extra-parameter tolerance

DeviceActions extracts only operation-applicable id/value/action; disconnect ignores these fields without forwarding them. Applicable values, operation and optional trusted fence retain validation.
